*!
*! SPLIT HH TAKE HH2 R AS HH2
J203  204                 M21(Q197)+30(2)=::2:
*! FOR EXIT PROXY, A SINGLE DEAD R SHOULD BE IN HH1 OR HH2
J203  204                 M21(Q197)+27(N1/3)=::1:

*! A MARRIED COUPLE BOTH DEAD SHOULD BE ONE IN HH1 AND ONE IN HH2
*! THE SCENARIO -- THE 1ST DEAD R DOES THE EXIT PROXY AND IS ASSIGNED
*! TO BE IN THE 2ND HH (WAS ASSUMED THAT SINCE THIS IS A DEAD R
*! AND THE R WAS MARRIED, THE SPOUSE WAS STILL LIVING).  GOING THROUGH
*! THAT RS INTERVIEW THEN MARKS OFF THIS SPOUSE (THE 2ND R) AS DEAD,
*! BUT IN THE 2ND HH.  SO FOR THIS R, IF MARKED AS DEAD IN THE 2ND HH,
*! THEN MEANS THAT THE OTHER R WENT THROUGH, AND THIS R IS IN THE 1ST HH.

*! IF THE RELATIONSHIP TO THE R IS SET TO LATE H/W/P, THEN THIS
*! A MARRIED COUPLE, AND THE R SHOULD THEN BE IN THE 2ND HH.
J203  204                 M21(Q197)+32(8/10)=::2:

*! OTHERWISE ASSUME 1ST HH
J203  204                 1(GE0.LE0)=::1:
